Motivation: 在压缩索引领域，许多经典字符串查询（随机访问、LCE、模式匹配、BWT、suffix array 等）已能在 O(|G|·log^O(1) n) 空间和 polylog n 时间内被高效支持。然而，频率相关查询（特别是大字母表上的 rank 查询和 symbol occurrence 查询）的复杂度仍缺乏精确下界。本文旨在填补这一空白，通过将这些查询归约到经典困难问题（BMM 和 OV），证明其本质上难以在压缩空间中高效回答。该问题的解决对于理解压缩索引的理论极限以及实际中大规模重复序列（如基因组数据）的处理具有重要意义。

Method: 方法核心是构造性归约：（1）对于 grammar 压缩上的 symbol occurrence 查询的硬度，通过从布尔矩阵 A、B 构造可压缩字符串 T_{A,B}：每行的 RowOnes 拼接形成块，使得 T_{A,B,i} 中符号 j 的出现等价于 (AB)[i,j]=1，从而将 BMM 转化为 n² 次符号存在查询；（2）对于 LZ78 压缩，利用"grammar boosting"技术添加前缀装置，使 LZ78 解析具有可控结构；（3）对于加性近似秩查询的硬度，使用 stretch_k 操作（将每个字符重复 k 次），使得 0 与 ≥k 的差距可通过近似值区分；（4）对于范围不同计数和众数频率查询，基于 OV 猜想，通过构造字符串 U_A 和 W_A，其中缺失元素对应正交对。所有构造的语法大小均为 O(n²) 或 O(nd)，可在 O(n²) 或 O(dn) 时间内构建。

Result: 主要结果包括：（1）若在 grammar 压缩文本上能以多项式对数时间批量回答 symbol occurrence 查询，则 BMM 可在 O(n²·log^O(1) n) 时间内求解（当前最佳为 O(n^{2.371339})）；（2）类似下界对 LZ78 压缩成立，即使 LZ78 是比 grammar 更弱的压缩模型；（3）即使仅需 N^{1-μ} 加性近似的双侧 rank 查询，仍可推出 BMM 的快速算法；（4）在 OV 猜想假设下，grammar 压缩上的 range distinct counting 和 range mode frequency 查询无法在 O(|G|^{1-ε}) 时间内支持；（5）作为推论，position-restricted pattern matching 查询也被证明是困难的。此外，论文还提供了 range distinct counting 的匹配上界 O(|G|·log n)，显示该下界基本紧致。

Conclusion: 本文通过精细的归约技术，将压缩频率相关查询与细粒度复杂性理论中的核心问题（BMM 和 OV）紧密联系起来，建立了一类新的频率相关查询的硬度屏障。结果表明，大字母表下 rank/symbol occurrence 查询的难度并非压缩索引的技术瓶颈，而是源自 BMM 等根本性困难问题的复杂性本质。此外，对 LZ78 压缩的扩展表明这些下界适用于更广泛的压缩框架，而 OV 猜想下的结果进一步将范围不同计数与众数频率查询纳入硬度版图。这些结论为未来设计压缩数据结构提供了重要的负面指引，提示研究者需重新考虑这些查询的可行参数空间，或转而寻找近似算法与受限情况的高效方案。

Abstract: Compressed indexing aims to support fundamental string queries in space proportional to compressed input size. For grammar compression, a length-$n$ string $T \in \Sigma^n$ represented by a grammar of size $|G|$ can support random access in $O(|G|\log^{O(1)} n)$ space and $O(\log^{O(1)} n)$ time, and the same bounds are known for many other queries, including pattern matching, longest common extension, lexicographic predecessor/successor, the Burrows-Wheeler transform, suffix arrays, and suffix trees. Frequency-related queries remain less understood. These include rank queries, which report the number of occurrences of a symbol $c \in \Sigma$ in a substring $T(b..e]$, and symbol occurrence queries, which ask whether $c$ occurs in $T(b..e]$. No fully general data structure is known for these queries with $O(|G|\log^{O(1)} n)$ space and $O(\log^{O(1)} n)$ query time. We establish new conditional lower bounds for such problems. First, we show that answering rank and symbol occurrence queries on grammar-compressed texts in polylogarithmic time using an $O(|G|\log^{O(1)} n)$-space structure constructible in $O(|G|\log^{O(1)} n)$ time would imply an $O(n^2\log^{O(1)} n)$-time algorithm for Boolean Matrix Multiplication. The proof uses a more general lower bound for efficiently answering a batch of such queries. Second, we extend the exact lower bounds from straight-line programs to LZ78-compressed strings, a weaker compression model. Third, independently, we show that even additive approximations of rank queries on straight-line grammars would imply faster Boolean Matrix Multiplication algorithms. Finally, assuming the Orthogonal Vectors conjecture, we show that other frequency-related problems, including range distinct counting and range mode frequency, also cannot be efficiently supported in compressed space.

</details>


### [207] [Revisiting Maximum $k$-Biplex Search Through $k$-Bounded-Degree Deletion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07419)
*Donghang Cui,Ronghua Li,Qiangqiang Dai,Guoren Wang*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的基于"删除"视角的最大 k-biplex 搜索算法 DMBP。通过揭示最大 k-biplex 与补图中最小 k-有界度删除之间的结构对偶性，设计了新的分支策略、分支规约技术和优化方法，在理论和实验上均显著优于现有最优算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最大 k-biplex 搜索问题是 NP-hard 问题，已有的精确算法在 k 值较大时（如 k≥3）效率急剧下降，在大规模二部图上表现不佳。需要一种从互补视角出发的更高效算法。

Method: 1. 揭示结构对偶性：最大 k-biplex 等价于在补图中找最小 k-有界度删除集（k-BDD）；2. 提出基于删除的分支策略 BranchBDD，选择补图中非度最大的顶点进行分支；3. 设计四种分支规约技术处理特殊情况（如 δ̄=k+1 时使用改进的 BranchImp）；4. 两种线性时间上界剪枝（基于顶点和基于集合）；5. 两阶段启发式算法提供高质量初始解（贪心删除 + 局部扩展）。

Result: 最坏时间复杂度为 O*(γ_k^n)，其中 γ_1=1.725, γ_2=1.856, γ_3=1.928，均优于现有算法。在 8 个真实二部图上的实验显示，DMBP 解决了全部 96 个测试用例，而 FastBB 和 CMBS-CSS 仅解决 23 和 22 个，速度提升最高达 4 个数量级。例如在 Aol 图上 k=4 时 6.14 秒完成，而基线算法 24 小时内无法完成。启发式解质量达到最优解的 85.2%-98.1%（平均 92.9%）。

Conclusion: 本文通过将最大 k-biplex 搜索问题转化为补图上的 k-有界度删除问题，提出了一种理论上和实践上都高效的算法 DMBP，达到了当前已知的最佳最坏时间复杂度，并通过优化技术实现了显著的实际加速。

Abstract: Biplex, as a relaxation of the biclique model, has emerged as an important cohesive subgraph model for bipartite graph analysis. The maximum $k$-biplex search problem aims to identify the $k$-biplex with maximum number of edges and has been widely applied in various real-world applications, including community detection, online recommendation, and fraud detection. However, the problem is NP-hard, and existing exact algorithms remain inefficient on large-scale bipartite graphs with large values of $k$ (e.g., $k\geq 3$). In this paper, we revisit the maximum $k$-biplex search problem from a complementary perspective. We reveal a novel structural duality: finding a maximum $k$-biplex in a bipartite graph is equivalent to finding a minimal $k$-bounded-degree deletion in its complement graph. Based on this observation, we propose a novel deletion-based algorithm for the maximum $k$-biplex search problem. We theoretically prove that the proposed algorithm achieves a worst-case time complexity of $O^*(\gamma_k^n)$, where $\gamma_k<2$. Specifically, $\gamma_1=1.725$, $\gamma_2=1.856$, and $\gamma_3=1.928$. To further enhance practical efficiency, we develop several effective upper-bounding techniques and a heuristic strategy for obtaining high-quality initial solutions, which substantially reduce the search space. Extensive experiments on eight real-world bipartite graphs demonstrate the efficiency of our approach, which achieves up to four orders of magnitude speedups over state-of-the-art algorithms.

</details>


### [208] [On the Assadi Liu Tarjan Auction Algorithm for Bipartite Matching: Simplification, Alternative Analysis, and Hard Instance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07439)
*Christian Konrad,Kheeran K. Naidu,Archie Walton,Eric Wang*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了 Assadi、Liu 和 Tarjan (SOSA'21) 提出的用于二部图最大匹配 (1-ε)-近似的多趟流式拍卖算法 (ALT-算法)，做出了三项贡献：(1) 证明了原算法中"冻结机制"是多余的，可直接去除；(2) 给出了基于增广路径的全新分析框架，自然地兼容去冻结版本；(3) 构造了首个需 Ω(1/ε²) 次迭代的硬实例——一个简单的路径图，表明该算法的 O(1/ε²) 迭代复杂度是紧的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ALT-算法在多趟半流式模型下是二部图匹配近似的最先进结果（O(1/ε²) 趟/(1-ε)-近似），但其原始分析使用组合拍卖中的势函数论证，对算法内部工作机制缺乏直观理解，且其正确性严格依赖"冻结机制"（一旦 B 侧顶点被重匹配 Θ(1/ε) 次就冻结其配对）。此外，是否存在需 Ω(1/ε²) 次迭代的硬实例仍是开放问题。这些不足促使作者重新审视该算法。

Method: 1. **算法简化**：将计数 c(b) 的增量从 ε 改为 1，并去除 Line 4 中的"c(b) ≤ 3/ε"冻结约束，使参数 ε 仅决定总迭代次数，从而支持 ε 的自适应细化。2. **增广路径分析**：建立 B-顶点 b 的重匹配次数 c(b) 与任何经过 b 的增广路径长度的联系——证明若存在从 a 出发的增广路径经过 b，则该路径长度 ≥ 2c(b)+1。由此证明引理 1：增广路径长度 k ≥ c_min(a₀)；并推出 M⊕OPT 中起点 c_min(a) ≥ k 的增广路径至多 |OPT|/(k+1) 条。利用此结构性结论证明推论 2（近似部分）和定理 3（进展部分），绕开势函数直接得到 (1-ε) 近似比。3. **硬实例构造**：在 6x−2 顶点的路径图上设 ε=1/(3x)，经过 4 轮"准备轮次"后进入"扫描"循环。每次扫描包含"右行"（沿路径向高索引推进）和"左行"（向低索引回退）两阶段，对索引 i > k 的每个 b_i 恰好重匹配 2 次，从而将所有这些 B 顶点的代价增加 2。对 A₂ 中 x 个顶点分别触发扫描，总迭代次数 Ω(x²)=Ω(n²)=Ω(1/ε²)。在 x ≥ 3 时任意 B 顶点代价 ≤ 3x−1 < 1/ε，因此冻结机制不被触发，证明对原算法同样成立。

Result: 1. 证明了冻结机制在 ALT-算法中完全可以移除，简化后的算法仅用 49/ε² 次迭代即可输出 (1-ε)-近似匹配（定理 3）。2. 增广路径分析揭示：算法本质上是在并行搜索增广路径，未匹配 A-顶点的 c_min 值即为从该点出发增广路径长度的下界；这将拍卖视角与传统增广路径视角统一起来。3. 在路径图上构造出首个 Ω(1/ε²) 迭代下界（定理 4 与推论 5），证明 ALT-算法的 O(1/ε²) 迭代复杂度是最优的；且该下界对原算法（含冻结机制）同样适用，因为冻结条件在该实例上永远不被触发。

Conclusion: 论文通过简化、重新分析和下界构造三个维度深化了对 ALT-拍卖算法的理解：冻结机制非必要、增广路径视角更具解释力、Ω(1/ε²) 迭代下界在简单路径图上即可达成。文末提出两个开放问题：(1) 是否存在仅依赖极大匹配计算、迭代次数严格少于 Θ(1/ε²) 的 (1-ε)-近似算法？(2) 能否证明此类算法的 Ω(1/ε) 迭代下界？

Abstract: Assadi, Liu, and Tarjan [SOSA'21] gave an auction algorithm that outputs a $(1-\epsilon)$-approximation to Maximum Matching in bipartite graphs. Their algorithm computes a sequence of $O(\frac{1}{\epsilon^2})$ maximal matchings in subgraphs of the input graph and can be implemented in the multi-pass streaming setting with $O(\frac{1}{\epsilon^2})$ passes in a straightforward manner, which constitutes the state-of-the-art pass/approximation trade-off result in the multi-pass streaming setting. Their analysis uses tools from combinatorial auctions and, at its heart, relies on a clever potential function argument. Their proof, however, provides only limited insight into the inner workings of the algorithm. In this paper, we revisit the ALT-algorithm and present the following contributions. Simplification: The ALT-algorithm is built upon a freezing mechanism where vertices on one side of the bipartition that have already been rematched $\Theta(\frac{1}{\epsilon})$ times over the course of the algorithm remain matched to their current partner forever. We show that this mechanism is in fact unnecessary, i.e., no special treatment of such vertices is needed. Alternative Analysis: We give an alternative analysis of the algorithm that is based on augmenting paths. Our analysis allows for a reinterpretation as one that follows the traditional approach of searching for and eliminating augmenting paths. Our analysis also copes with the removal of the freezing mechanism in a natural way, whereas the analysis of Assadi et al. strictly depends on its use. Hard Instance: We provide the first hard instance on which the algorithm requires $\Omega(\frac{1}{\epsilon^2})$ iterations/maximal matching computations. The instance is a simple path graph, where we exhibit a cyclic behaviour that prevents fast progress.

</details>


### [209] [From Decision to Random Certificates: Exponential Separation for Edge Estimation with Independent Set Queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07483)
*Debarshi Chanda,Buddha Dev Das,Arijit Ghosh,Gopinath Mishra*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的查询模型ISC（Independent Set with Certificate），用于在子线性查询复杂度下估计无向无权图的边数。通过将标准的独立集（IS）查询增强为返回均匀随机边的"随机证书"，作者给出了一个仅需Õ(log²m)次查询即可获得(1±ε)-近似边数估计的随机算法，相比标准IS查询（需要Õ(min{√m, n/√m})）和全局随机边采样（需要Õ(√m)）实现了指数级分离。算法的关键创新是引入了"近似保持子集/序列"的概念，结合图稀疏化技术（Doob鞅）和生日悖论估计器，构造了一条长度期望为O(log m)的嵌套顶点子集链，使得每一层保留常数比例的边数，从而实现了与顶点数n无关、仅依赖于边数m的对数级查询复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在子线性图算法中，边数估计是一个核心问题。现有的查询模型——包括局部查询（如度数、邻居查询）、独立集(IS)查询、双独立集(BIS)查询以及全局随机边采样——各自存在查询复杂度的下界限制。特别是Chen等人(2020)已证明标准IS查询需要Õ(min{√m, n/√m})次查询，这给出了边数估计在IS模型下的紧界。本文的核心动机是：能否通过对决策式IS查询增加最少的额外信息（如随机证书）来突破这一查询复杂度下界？这一研究连接了群组测试、决策vs计数二分法、图性质测试以及"随机证书的威力"等多个理论计算机科学主题。

Method: 方法核心是构造"近似保持序列"(Approximation-Preserving Sequence)：
1. **查询模型ISC**：给定顶点子集U⊆V，若U的导出子图非空，则返回该子图中一条均匀随机边；否则返回null。这保留了IS查询的结构约束，仅增强返回信息。
2. **阈值检查（IsCollision + BirthdayEstimate）**：基于生日悖论，通过检测随机边采样中的碰撞来区分m(U)≤τ与m(U)≥8τ两种情况；利用碰撞数估计器C(s,2)/r近似估计小边数。
3. **图稀疏化（ApproxSubset）**：将顶点子集U随机二分为U¹和U²，利用Doob鞅分析证明总边数近似为m(U)/2（误差为Õ(√m(U))）；通过采样O(log(1/δ))条随机边，使用Chernoff界识别保留常数比例边的"好部分"。
4. **近似保持序列构造（ApproxSequence）**：迭代应用稀疏化与阈值检查，得到U₀⊇U₁⊇...⊇U_κ，每层满足1/32 ≤ m(Uᵢ)/m(Uᵢ₋₁) ≤ 3/4，期望长度κ=O(log m)。
5. **乘法重建（EdgeEstimation）**：估计每层比值pᵢ = m(Uᵢ)/m(Uᵢ₋₁)，利用pᵢ=Ω(1)的性质只需O(log κ/ε²)次查询；最终估计m̂ = m̂_b / ∏p̂ᵢ，通过Hoeffding界累积误差分析获得(1±ε)-近似。

Result: 主要结果（定理1）：存在随机算法，使用ISC查询，以至少2/3的概率输出边数m的(1±ε)-近似，期望查询复杂度为O(ε⁻² log²m · log log m)。这一结果实现了：
1. 与标准IS查询的指数级分离：后者需要Õ(min{√m, n/√m}) = Õ(√n)次查询；
2. 与全局随机边采样模型的指数级分离：后者需要Õ(√m)次样本；
3. 输出敏感性的查询复杂度：对n无依赖（即使在低阶项中），仅依赖于m；
4. 改进了基于BIS访问的Õ(log⁵n)算法（Addanki等人）。

Conclusion: 本文通过引入ISC查询模型（独立集查询+随机证书），证明了在边数估计问题上，相比标准IS查询和全局随机边采样可以实现指数级的查询复杂度改进，达到Õ(log²m)的多对数级复杂度。技术核心是"近似保持序列"的构造，它将问题转化为对一系列常数比例边保留的子集进行迭代约简，并通过对局部比值的乘法估计完成最终重构。该模型在概念上连接了群组测试、决策vs计数二分法、条件采样和随机证书理论，展示了在图查询模型中增加最小额外信息（随机证书）可以根本性地改变子线性估计算法的复杂度景观。这为未来研究开辟了新方向：(1) ISC查询在其他图问题（如三角形估计、连通性）中的应用；(2) 决策式查询与随机证书结合的更一般框架；(3) 查询复杂度的紧下界分析。

Abstract: We study the problem of estimating the number of edges in an undirected, unweighted graph using sublinear query access. We consider a query model that preserves the structure of Independent Set (IS) queries, but augments their output with a random certificate: given a vertex subset, the oracle returns a uniformly random edge from the induced subgraph if one exists, and returns null otherwise. Using this access, we give a randomized algorithm that outputs a $(1 \pm \varepsilon)$-approximation to the number of edges with constant success probability using $\widetilde{O}(\log^{2} m)$ queries. This implies an exponential separation from both standard IS queries and global random edge-sampling models: estimating the number of edges using standard IS queries require $\widetilde{\Theta}\!\left(\min\left\{\sqrt{m},\, \frac{n}{\sqrt{m}}\right\}\right)$ queries, while direct random edge-sample access requires $\widetilde{\Theta}(\sqrt{m})$ samples. Beyond separation in query complexity, our algorithm is output-sensitive: its query complexity is polylogarithmic in the number of edges in the graph. This aligns with the classical objective in group testing, where one seeks algorithms that are both worst-case optimal and instance-adaptive. Conceptually, our model connects group testing, the decision-versus-counting dichotomy, graph property testing, and the "power of a random certificate", and can be viewed as a structured form of conditional sampling of edges in graphs.

</details>


### [210] [Stochastic Online Euclidean TSP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07537)
*Daniel Anker Hermansen*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文针对在线欧几里得旅行商问题（Online Euclidean TSP），提出了一种简单的确定性算法，在$d\geq 2$维时达到$O(1)$的期望竞争比，在$d=1$维时达到$O(\log n)$的期望竞争比（匹配下界），并在$1+\varepsilon$松弛时间槽的$d=1$情形下达到$O(1+\log\varepsilon^{-1})$的期望竞争比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线欧几里得TSP中，点逐个揭示且必须立即分配时间槽且不可重新分配。之前的工作：Bertram（ESA 2025）在最坏情况下实现了$O(\sqrt{n})$的竞争比；Kalavas、Platanos、Tolias（STACS 2026）在随机输入下实现了$O(\log^2 n)$的竞争比。本文动机是在随机在线欧几里得TSP设置下，缩小与最优离线解之间的差距，实现更好的（甚至常数级别的）期望竞争比。

Method: 作者提出了一种简单（simple）的确定性算法，其竞争比的期望值来源于随机输入本身。对于$d\geq 2$的均匀采样输入，设计算法使得期望竞争比为$O(1)$；对于$d=1$（即一维排序问题的推广），期望竞争比为$O(\log n)$，该上界与Hu（SODA 2026）给出的下界匹配。在有$\lceil(1+\varepsilon)n\rceil$个时间槽的$d=1$变体中，算法实现$O(1+\log\varepsilon^{-1})$的期望竞争比。文章还综述了相关文献中的算法。

Result: 主要结果包括：(1) $d\geq 2$时随机在线欧几里得TSP的期望竞争比为$O(1)$，显著优于之前的$O(\log^2 n)$；(2) $d=1$时达到$O(\log n)$，与Hu（SODA 2026）的下界匹配；(3) $d=1$且时间槽数为$\lceil(1+\varepsilon)n\rceil$时，竞争比为$O(1+\log\varepsilon^{-1})$。实验评估表明，该算法在所有变体中渐近常数因子较小，并且与其他文献算法进行了比较。

Conclusion: 本文为随机在线欧几里得TSP问题提供了简单且近乎最优的算法。在高维情况下实现了常数级别的期望竞争比，在一维情况下达到了匹配下界的对数竞争比。算法的简洁性和良好的实验性能表明其在理论和实践上的双重价值。文章同时综述了该领域相关算法的发展脉络。

Abstract: In the Euclidean travelling salesman problem (Euclidean TSP), a salesman must visit $n$ points in Euclidean space, while minimizing the travel distance, according to the Euclidean distance function. In online Euclidean TSP, introduced by Abrahamsen, Bercea, Beretta, Klausen and Kozma [ESA 2024], the points are revealed one at a time, and a time slot must be assigned before the next is revealed. Once a point is assigned to a time slot, it can never be reassigned to another time slot. There are $n$ time slots. Euclidean online TSP is a high-dimensional generalization of online sorting, introduced by Aamand, Abrahamsen, Beretta and Kleist [SODA 2023]. Bertram [ESA 2025] showed an algorithm that achieves a competitive ratio of $O(\sqrt{n})$ in the worst case. In stochastic online Euclidean TSP, the points are sampled uniformly and independently in the unit $d$-cube. Kalavas, Platanos and Tolias [STACS 2026] presented an algorithm achieving a competitive ratio of $O(\log^2 n)$ with high probability for stochastic online Euclidean TSP. We present a simple algorithm that for $d \geq 2$ achieves an expected competitive ratio of $O(1)$, and for $d=1$ achieves an expected competitive ratio of $O(\log n)$, matching the lower bound by Hu [SODA 2026] for $d=1$. The algorithm is deterministic, and the expectation is due to the stochastic input. We also show that in the variant where there are more time slots than points, i.e., $\left\lceil(1 + \varepsilon)n\right\rceil$ time slots and $d=1$, our algorithm achieves an expected competitive ratio of $O\left(1 + \log \varepsilon^{-1}\right)$. We also survey algorithms from the literature. We experimentally evaluate our algorithm, which reveals that in all variants the constant factor hidden asymptotically is small. We also evaluate the algorithms from the literature.

</details>


### [211] [On Computing Minimum Wheeler DFA From Their Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07563)
*Ruben Becker,Davide Cenzato,Nicola Prezza,Daniel Puttini*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个针对一般DFA（确定性有限自动机）计算最小Wheeler DFA的近最优算法，时间复杂度为输出敏感的线性对数级 O(m_w log m_w)，填补了此前仅限无环DFA的高效算法与可处理一般拓扑但极慢的算法之间的空白，并在泛基因组学图谱数据上验证了实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Wheeler自动机是Burrows-Wheeler变换（BWT）在标记图上的推广，可实现压缩图上的最优线性时间模式匹配，在计算泛基因组学中具有重要应用。然而，已知在NFA输入下计算最小Wheeler DFA是PSPACE-hard问题。对于DFA输入，先前最优算法存在两难：Alanko等（SODA 2020）的O(m_w log m_w)算法仅限无环DFA；D'Agostino等（TCS 2023）首次支持一般DFA但需O(n³n_w + n²σn_w log n_w)时间，实际不可用。如何同时获得高效率与一般性成为核心瓶颈。

Method: 算法以输入DFA的源点为根构建生成树，并对该树按到达路径字符串的逆字典序排序，得到一个Wheeler子图D_w及队列Δ（包含未纳入D_w的转移）。迭代从Δ取出转移(u,a,v)，若加入D_w不违反Wheeler公理则直接加入（情形1）；否则根据前驱p与后继s的关系分两种处理（情形2: p=s≠⊥；情形3: p≠s或s=⊥）。核心操作是创建Myhill-Nerode等价的副本状态（通过copy函数），将v的相关转移复制到新状态v'，并按Wheeler公理将v'精确插入LEX（Wheeler序）的合适位置，使D_w保持Wheeler性、不变式持续成立。算法使用动态数据结构（基于Nekrich-Navarro的动态字符串、有序位图和偏和数据结构）维护(LEX, λ', IN, OUT)四元组表示WDFA，支持对数时间的查询与更新。

Result: 主要定理（定理1.1）：给定最小DFA D接受Wheeler语言L，可在 O(m_w log m_w) 时间内构造具有m_w条转移的最小Wheeler DFA D_w。结合Becker等的二次时间Wheeler性判定算法，可对任意DFA输入终止并返回结果。正确性证明依赖于7条算法不变式（确定性、可达性、Wheeler序、Myhill-Nerode等价、语言保持、Wheeler最小性、共可达性），利用反证法证明算法当且仅当语言为Wheeler时终止。运行时分析显示D_w中状态和转移仅增不减，每次操作对数时间。实验在23个人类染色体泛基因组图上验证：吞吐率超过10^5 transitions/second，与唯一同类工具GCSA时间相当但产出保证最小性，并对GCSA因内存溢出失败的染色体18完成了构造。

Conclusion: 本文首次实现了针对一般DFA输入计算最小Wheeler DFA的近最优线性对数级算法，统一了Alanko等与D'Agostino等的优势，时间复杂度较后者提升至少一个二次因子。该算法为泛基因组学等应用提供了可证明最优的模式匹配数据结构构建工具，是Burrows-Wheeler变换在图结构数据上的实用化重要进展。未来工作包括优化大规模自动机下的数据结构以进一步提升性能。

Abstract: Wheeler automata have recently emerged as a powerful generalization of the Burrows-Wheeler Transform, enabling optimal linear-time pattern matching on compressed labeled graphs -- a task that is otherwise computationally hard. Consequently, when an automaton recognizes a Wheeler language (i.e., it is equivalent to some Wheeler automaton), computing its minimum equivalent Wheeler DFA is a powerful indexing strategy. This problem is particularly relevant in computational pangenomics, where pangenome graphs frequently recognize Wheeler languages. However, constructing the minimum Wheeler DFA for a Wheeler language has remained a computational bottleneck. The problem is known to be PSPACE-hard for nondeterministic inputs. When the input is a DFA, state-of-the-art solutions forced a compromise: they were either fast but limited to acyclic DFAs (Alanko et al., SODA 2020) or capable of handling general topologies but prohibitively slow (D'Agostino et al., TCS 2023). In this work, we bridge this gap with the first algorithm solving the problem for general DFAs in near-optimal, linearithmic output-sensitive time. By matching the efficiency of acyclic-only solutions while retaining full generality, our approach improves upon the previous general solution by at least a quadratic factor. We demonstrate the practical impact of our algorithm on real-world pangenome graphs; our tool achieves a processing throughput of over 10^5 transitions per second on a standard workstation, enabling the construction of a provably optimal pattern matching data structure in such applications.

</details>


### [212] [Unconditional Lower Bounds for Degree Fault Tolerant Spanners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07576)
*Greg Bodwin,Aleksey Lopez*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文研究了度数容错（f-DFT）乘法图spanner的无条件下界，证明了存在n节点的图使得任何f-DFT (2k-1)-拉伸spanner至少需要 Ω(f^{1-1/k}·n^{1+1/k}) 条边。这消除了之前结果对Erdős围长猜想(girth conjecture)的依赖，并通过分析Wenger图给出了显式构造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 1. 经典spanner的紧致上下界长期依赖于1963年Erdős提出的围长猜想(girth conjecture)，而该猜想仅在k∈{1,2,3,5}时得到证实，且学界近年来对其普遍性产生怀疑（如Conlon猜测ex(n,C₈)=o(n^{5/4})，意味着k=4时围长猜想可能为假）。<br>2. 现有容错spanner模型（顶点/边容错、彩色容错）的下界均通过归约至ex(n,C_{≤2k})，因此无条件紧下界的证明等价于围长猜想本身，难度极大。<br>3. 度数容错(DFT)模型由Bodwin、Haeupler和Parter提出，允许每个节点最多f条边故障，总故障量可达O(nf)，是更宽松的故障模型；此前其下界仍依赖围长猜想。本文旨在探索DFT模型是否强大到足以绕过围长猜想获得无条件下界。

Method: 1. 采用Wenger图（1991）的代数构造，以Szabó和Conlon（2021）的代数重新诠释为基础，构造点-线关联系统：<br>   • 点集为𝔽_p^k，线集由斜率向量σ_t=(1,t,t²,…,t^{k-1})生成；<br>   • 关联图ℬ为二部图，具有2n节点和n^{1+1/k}条边。<br>2. 对每条边e={a,ℓ}定义故障集F_e：包含所有与ℓ平行线上、且可通过ℓ-横截路径（line-length ≤ k/2-1）从a到达的点所对应的边。<br>3. 利用Vandermonde矩阵分析（Lemma 9）证明短闭合游走中每条斜率至少出现两次，从而保证：<br>   • F_e∪{e}的最大度为1（Lemma 11）；<br>   • 每条长度为≤2k的过e的环必包含F_e中的边（Lemma 13），证明依赖于"路径拉直"技术（Lemma 12）。<br>4. 由此得到ℬ的唯一1-DFT (2k-1)-spanner是其自身（Theorem 14）。<br>5. 通过标准的"云膨胀"(cloud blowup)技术，将每个节点替换为f个副本，每条边替换为完全二部图，将结果推广至任意f≥1，得到ℬ^{⟨f⟩}为唯一f-DFT (2k-1)-spanner（Theorem 19）。

Result: 主要定理：对于所有正整数k，存在n节点输入图G，使得任何f-DFT (2k-1)-spanner H必须满足 |E(H)| ≥ Ω(f^{1-1/k}·n^{1+1/k})。<br>• 这是首个针对乘法spanner变体的无条件紧下界（仅差O_k(1)因子），不依赖围长猜想；<br>• 与Bodwin-Haeupler-Parter的上界O(exp(k)·f^{1-1/k}·n^{1+1/k})基本匹配；<br>• 与Lazebnik-Ustimenko-Woldar（1995）的已知无条件下界相比，代表了多项式量级的改进；<br>• 同时首次证明Wenger图捕获了已知代数技术与证明围长猜想所需结构之间的差距。

Conclusion: 度数容错spanner是乘法spanner中首个能够获得无条件近最优下界的变体。该结果表明DFT模型在某种意义上"绕过"了围长猜想的困难——虽然Wenger图本身含有短环，但其短环在图中稀疏分布，可被低度故障集破坏。本文的方法将Szabó和Conlon对Wenger图的代数重新诠释形式化，为在无需围长猜想条件下分析spanner下界提供了新思路，并有望启发对其他spanner变体的无条件下界研究。

Abstract: We study multiplicative graph spanners in the $f$-degree fault tolerant ($f$-DFT) model, in which the spanner must approximately preserve distances even after any subset of edges of maximum degree $f$ temporarily "fails" and is removed from the graph. We prove that there are $n$-node lower bound graphs for which any $f$-DFT $(2k-1)$-stretch spanner $H$ must have size $$|E(H)| \ge \Omega\left( f^{1-1/k} n^{1+1/k}\right).$$ This matches a lower bound that was previously only known to hold conditionally, under the 1963 girth conjecture of Erdős. It also matches the current upper bounds, up to a factor of $\texttt{exp}(k)$. Our proof is an analysis of the so-called Wenger graphs (J. Comb. Theory 1991), via their recent reinterpretation by Szabó and by Conlon (Am. Math. Monthly 2021).

</details>


### [213] [Approximability of Electrical Distribution Network Reconfiguration for General Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07600)
*Christian Wallisch,Andrea Benigni,Carsten Hartmann,Leon Kellerhals*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文研究配电网重构（DNR）问题的可近似性，即在具有冗余线路的电网中寻找使电阻性功率损耗最小化的生成树。作者针对不同源数量场景给出了多个近似上界和不可近似下界，改进了单源均匀电阻情形的最优近似比，并证明了多源情形下的强不可近似性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 配电网通常以辐射状（生成树）方式运行以降低损耗，但寻找最优辐射状配置是NP难的，且随着低碳技术（光伏、电动车、热泵）带来的负荷模式变化，需要频繁快速重构。然而现有理论结果有限：1-DNR仅有O(n)近似，且多源情形的可近似性基本未被研究。本文旨在系统刻画DNR问题在不同源数设定下的可近似性边界。

Method: 1) 将1-DNR等价为无容量汇流（confluent flow）问题，利用随机舍入方法对分裂流（splittable flow）的QP松弛进行舍入，通过耦合论证（coupling argument）分析路径重叠的期望长度；2) 通过Quadratic Load Balancing的L-归约证明1-DNR的APX-困难性；3) 将多源DNR归约到Transshipment问题获得(n-1)-近似；4) 通过证明平面图上固定k=2的Balanced Connected Partition为NP-完全（解决Dyer-Frieze 1985年猜想）进而得到多源DNR的n^(1-ε)不可近似性；5) 通过Set Cover的近似保持归约证明2-DNR的Ω(log²n)不可近似性。

Result: 主要结果包括：(1) 1-DNR在均匀电阻下存在O(√n)随机近似算法（回答Gupta等人的开放问题）；(2) 1-DNR是APX-困难的，即使电阻为0/1、需求多项式有界；(3) DNR存在(n-1)-近似算法；(4) 即使在平面图上，DNR也不存在n^(1-ε)-近似（对任意ε>0），除非P=NP；(5) 2-DNR不存在c·log²n-近似，除非P=NP。方法上证明了BCP在平面图k=2时NP-完全，解决了Dyer-Frieze的猜想。

Conclusion: 本文基本刻画了DNR问题在单源、双源和多源设定下的可近似性全景。单源均匀电阻情形的O(√n)与下界之间仍有较大差距，呼吁进一步研究；多源情形的上界与下界基本吻合。未来工作可考虑结合树宽、反馈边数等图结构参数，以及引入热限制、电压下界等实际操作约束，甚至考虑通过序列开关动作实现重构的动态场景。

Abstract: Electrical distribution networks are regional, medium- and low-voltage power grids connecting energy sources to individual households and businesses with given power demands. While these networks contain redundant power lines for reliability, they are typically operated in a radial (spanning tree) configuration by opening and closing switches on the lines. The challenge is to find a spanning tree that minimizes the sum of the resistive power losses: The power loss of a line $e$ is its resistance $r(e)$ times the squared current $f(e)^2$ flowing across the line. We study approximation algorithms for this problem, known as Distribution Network Reconfiguration (DNR). We give an $n$-approximation algorithm and, via a new NP-hardness for planar Balanced Connected Partition with a fixed number of parts, show that no $n^{1-\varepsilon}$-approximation is possible even on planar graphs unless P $=$ NP, for any $\varepsilon>0$. Since the approximation hardness holds only if there are many sources, we focus on $k$-DNR with $k$ sources; this is motivated by traditional distribution networks, where oftentimes $k = 1$. For $2$-DNR, we give an approximation lower bound of $\Omega(\log^2 n)$ conditioned on P $\neq$ NP. For $1$-DNR, which is equivalent to finding an uncapacitated confluent flow minimizing the squared Euclidean norm, we prove APX-hardness and give an $\mathcal{O}(\sqrt{n})$-approximation for uniform line resistances, answering an open question by Gupta et al. [Math. Program. 2022].

</details>


### [214] [Faster Randomized and Deterministic k-Clustering on Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07615)
*Sebastian Forster,Yasamin Nazari,Rajath Rao K. N.,Antonis Skarlatos*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文针对图上的k-聚类问题提出了更快的随机化和确定性算法。三个主要贡献：(1)首个确定性的图上k-center (2+ε)-近似 Õ(m)时间算法（解决了Abboud等人的公开问题）；(2)随机化的(k,z)-聚类 O(1)-近似 Õ(m)时间算法，统一覆盖k-median和k-means；(3)确定性的(k,z)-聚类 O(poly(c))-近似 Õ(m^{1+1/c})时间算法，通过建立与Thorup-Zwick距离预言机的技术联系实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图上的k-聚类（k-center、k-median、k-means）在网络分析、聚类挖掘中有广泛应用。现有随机化算法对k-median已能实现近线性时间，但确定性算法和一般z值的(k,z)-聚类在近线性时间方面仍有显著空白。Abboud等人明确将"确定性的近线性时间k-center算法"列为公开问题。本文的核心动机是缩小图上k-聚类算法在运行时间上接近线性（m）的差距，特别是在确定性和一般z值方面。

Method: 核心技术工具是Górkiewicz-Karczmarz的源点插入(source-insertion)动态(1+ε)-SSSP数据结构。三个算法均基于此：(1) k-center：在Gonzalez贪心框架中，通过添加超源点s并向每个选中的中心插入零权边(s, c_i)，利用源点插入SSSP维护到当前中心集的距离估计，从而在O~(m/ε)时间内找到距离当前中心集最远的顶点；(2) 随机化(k,z)-聚类：基于Dupré la Tour-Saulpic的递归贪心模板（源于Mettu-Plaxton框架），用Cohen的随机化球大小估计算法实现Value原语，用截断Dijkstra和源点插入SSSP实现近似球和禁止(forbidding)循环，通过证明每条边在每个半径尺度上至多被松弛两次来获得Õ(m)总时间；(3) 确定性(k,z)-聚类：核心创新是用Thorup-Zwick距离预言机中的bunch和cluster结构来确定性估计球大小，将近似邻域N(v,r)定义为截断的bunch与各层pivot的截断cluster的并集（至多t+1个集合），利用Bunch-Cluster对偶性和距离伸缩性证明B(v,r)⊆N(v,r)⊆B(v, 2αr)，其中α=4t-2；预处理时间为O~(t·m·n^{1/t})。

Result: 主要定理：(1) 定理1.1：确定性的增量式k-center算法，在O~(m/ε)时间内达到(2+ε)-近似；在加权路径图上由Kozma证明的2-下界表明此结果对增量算法在加性ε意义下是最优的。(2) 定理1.2：常数k下的k-center带离群点问题，O~(m/ε)时间内以(1-t/n)·(ε/⌈1+ε⌉)^{k-1}概率获得(2+ε)-近似，使用至多(1+ε)t个离群点；引理26给出一般k的双准则结果。(3) 定理1.3：随机化增量式(k,z)-聚类算法在Õ(m log Δ)时间内以高概率获得O(1)-近似，统一了Thorup关于k-median的结果和Jiang等人的m^{1+o(1)}结果。(4) 定理1.4：确定性增量式(k,z)-聚类算法，在Õ(t·m·n^{1/t} + m/ε)时间内获得O(poly(t))-近似。所有算法均支持增量式输出（即前ℓ个中心构成ℓ-聚类的常数近似解）。

Conclusion: 本文在图上k-聚类问题的算法效率方面取得了重要进展：(1) 解决了一个具体的公开问题——提供了首个确定性的近线性时间k-center算法；(2) 将随机化(k,z)-聚类的运行时间从m^{1+o(1)}改进到近线性Õ(m)，统一覆盖k-median和k-means；(3) 通过建立聚类算法与Thorup-Zwick距离预言机之间出乎意料的技术联系，为确定性(k,z)-聚类提供了首个接近线性的算法，这一技术联系具有独立的研究价值。这些结果缩小了图上k-聚类接近线性时间运行时间的理论与实践之间的差距。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the $(k,z)$-clustering and $k$-center problems on graphs, where $(k,z)$-clustering generalizes the $k$-median ($z=1$) and $k$-means ($z=2$) problems. We obtain the following main results. Our first contribution is the first deterministic algorithm for $k$-center on graphs that achieves a $(2+\varepsilon)$-approximation in $\tilde{O}(m)$ time. This affirmatively resolves an open problem raised by Abboud, Cohen-Addad, Lee, and Manurangsi [SOSA 2023]. Our techniques also extend to the $k$-center with outliers problem, where up to $t$ points may be discarded. Our second contribution is a randomized algorithm for $(k,z)$-clustering on graphs that achieves an $O(1)$-approximation in $\tilde{O}(m)$ time, which in particular covers $k$-median ($z=1$) and $k$-means ($z=2$). Prior to this work, an $\tilde{O}(m)$-time randomized algorithm was known for $k$-median by Thorup [SIAM J. Comput. 2005], and a recent work of Jiang, Jin, Lou, and Lu [2026] achieves $m^{1+o(1)}$ time for general $z$ via local search. Finally, we design a deterministic algorithm for $(k,z)$-clustering on graphs that achieves an $O(\mathrm{poly}(c))$-approximation in $\tilde{O}(m^{1+1/c})$ time, for a positive parameter $c$. To obtain this result, we use techniques from the Thorup-Zwick distance oracle [JACM 2005]; this technical connection may be of independent interest, considering the wide application of distance oracles in various computational settings. Most of our algorithms are incremental, in the sense that for any given parameter $k$, they return a sequence of centers such that every prefix of length $\ell \leq k$ yields a constant-factor approximate solution to the $\ell$-clustering problem.

</details>


### [215] [Pure Nash Equilibria in Graphical Games of Bounded Width Revisited](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07627)
*Michael Lampis,Yiren Lu*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了图形博弈中纯纳什均衡（PNE）存在性问题的参数化复杂度。研究指出Thomas和van Leeuwen [2015] 提出的 $\alpha^{O(\mathrm{tw})}$ 算法存在根本性错误，进而在tw、pw、ctw三种图宽度参数下给出了改进算法及对应的紧下界，证明了路径宽度和切割宽度下的算法在pw-SETH假设下是最优的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图形博弈中PNE存在性判定是NP完全问题，参数化复杂度是处理该问题的标准工具。此前Gottlob等人和Daskalakis-Papadimitriou给出了参数依赖为 $\alpha^{(\Delta+1)\mathrm{tw}}$ 的自然动态规划算法。Thomas与van Leeuwen [2015] 声称可将参数依赖改进为 $\alpha^{O(\mathrm{tw})}$，该结论被后续工作广泛引用，但本文发现该结论是错误的——若其成立将蕴含FPT=W[1]。因此需要精确刻画该问题在标准图参数下的细粒度复杂度。

Method: 方法包括三个层面：(1) 通过从Multicolored Clique问题的参数化归约，证明即使在α=2时PNE判定问题关于顶点覆盖（从而关于树宽）也是W[1]-难的，从而否定Thomas-van Leeuwen的算法；(2) 提出新的组合引理，通过"轻/重"邻居的二分技术和切换叶子的构造，将图G的共邻图 $G^{\mathrm{co}}$（推广的图平方）的宽度与G的宽度和最大出度联系起来，将系数从 $(\Delta+1)$ 改进为 $\lfloor 2\Delta/3+1\rfloor$（树宽）或 $\lfloor \Delta/2+1\rfloor$（路径宽度）；(3) 通过将PNE判定归约为CSP实例（基图为 $G^{\mathrm{co}}$），结合CSP已知算法获得上界；通过从适当字母表的CSP反归约并利用网格构造（基于枢纽顶点）将路径宽度压缩，证明pw-SETH下的紧下界。

Result: 主要结果包括：(1) 即使α=2时，PNE判定问题关于顶点覆盖（从而关于树宽）是W[1]-难的，纠正了Thomas-van Leeuwen的错误声明；(2) 新算法：$\alpha^{\lfloor 2\Delta/3+1\rfloor \cdot \mathrm{tw}}|\mathcal{G}|^{O(1)}$（树宽）、$\alpha^{\lfloor \Delta/2+1\rfloor \cdot \mathrm{pw}}|\mathcal{G}|^{O(1)}$（路径宽度）、$\alpha^{\mathrm{ctw}}|\mathcal{G}|^{O(1)}$（切割宽度），均改进了原先的 $\alpha^{(\Delta+1)\mathrm{tw}}$ 依赖；(3) 关键技术工具——关于 $G^{\mathrm{co}}$ 宽度的改进组合上界及其构造性证明，附带关于无向图 $G^2$ 宽度的推论；(4) 紧下界：关于路径宽度和切割宽度的算法改进等价于推翻pw-SETH，证明了这些算法的最优性；(5) 通过无穷图族证明组合上界中的常数因子 $1/2$ 和 $2/3$ 本质上是最优的。

Conclusion: 本文确立了PNE计算的结构参数化复杂度的精确图景：纠正了文献中的错误声明，给出了关于树宽、路径宽度、切割宽度的最优算法（后两者在pw-SETH假设下），并提供了一个关于图平方宽度关系的有用组合工具。最核心的开放问题是树宽与路径宽度之间复杂度差距——$1/2$ 与 $2/3$ 的不同系数——这在参数化复杂度中极为罕见。另一个开放问题是将路径宽度的紧下界推广到偶数度情形。

Abstract: We revisit the complexity of deciding whether a graphical game admits a pure Nash equilibrium (PNE) parameterized by standard measures of the input graph, such as treewidth. The natural dynamic programming algorithm for this problem has parameter dependence $\alpha^{(\Delta+1)\text{tw}}$ where $\alpha$ is the maximum number of strategies available to each player, each player's utility depends on at most $\Delta$ other players, and the input graph has width $\text{tw}$. Our first contribution is to point out that an algorithm by Thomas and van Leeuwen [Algorithmica 2015] claiming to improve this dependence to $\alpha^{O(\text{tw})}$ is flawed and, more strongly, such an algorithm would imply that FPT=W[1]. We then set out to pinpoint the fine-grained complexity of this problem with respect to standard parameters and show that the natural DP algorithm is not optimal, as the problem can be solved with dependence $\alpha^{\lfloor \frac{2\Delta}{3} + 1 \rfloor \text{tw}}$, $\alpha^{\lfloor \frac{\Delta}{2} + 1 \rfloor \text{pw}}$, and $\alpha^{\text{ctw}}$, where $\text{pw}, \text{ctw}$ are the pathwidth and cutwidth of the input respectively. Our main algorithmic tool is a tightening of the relationship between the width of a graph $G$, its maximum degree, and the width of $G^2$, which may be of independent interest. Complementing these results, we show that our algorithms for pathwidth and cutwidth are likely to be optimal, as improving them is equivalent to falsifying the pw-SETH.

</details>


### [216] [Exploiting Spanning Trees for Directed Acyclicity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07705)
*Sergei Khargeliia,Danil Sagunov*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文研究有向图上加权最大无环子图（MAS）问题，针对最大生成树（MaxST）下界提出两个参数化算法：对于整数权重情况运行时间为 $2^{k^{\mathcal{O}(1)}}\cdot \mathcal{I}^{\mathcal{O}(1)}$，对有理数权重（≥1）情况运行时间为 $n^{k^{\mathcal{O}(1)}}\cdot \mathcal{I}^{\mathcal{O}(1)}$，可找到总权值至少比 MaxST 多 $k$ 的无环子图，否则报告不存在。这是加权有向图问题上少数的"超越保证"正面结果之一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典 MAS 问题的两个著名下界——随机排序下界（保证至少总权一半）和最大生成树（MaxST）下界——在 MaxST 覆盖较多总权值的实例上，MaxST 优于随机排序。然而，在 MaxST 之上进一步寻找更优无环子图的能力在参数化复杂度意义下尚未被研究。值得注意的是，对随机排序下界而言，即使在有理权值下，$k=1$ 时的"超越保证"已是 NP-难问题，这使得 MaxST 下界上的超越保证研究更具理论与算法价值。

Method: 提出两个核心算法：(1) 整数权值情形：以参数 $k$（超出 MaxST 的目标权值增量）为核心，在 $2^{k^{\mathcal{O}(1)}}\cdot \mathcal{I}^{\mathcal{O}(1)}$ 时间内求解；(2) 有理权值（≥1）情形：在 $n^{k^{\mathcal{O}(1)}}\cdot \mathcal{I}^{\mathcal{O}(1)}$ 时间内求解。关键技术包括：剖析最大生成树结构与有向圈之间的内在联系，并利用完美图定理（perfect graph theorem）处理有理权值情形。方法本质上将"超越 MaxST"问题转化为参数化搜索/优化问题。

Result: 理论上证明存在两个分别针对整数权值与有理权值情形的固定参数可解（FPT）算法，能在相应运行时间内输出权值至少为 $\text{MaxST}(G)+k$ 的无环子图或判定其不存在。研究同时揭示了：(i) MaxST 与有向圈结构之间未曾被发掘的深刻联系；(ii) 完美图理论在加权参数化算法中的新应用；(iii) 即使在 $k=1$ 的最小参数下，相对于随机排序下界是 NP-难，但相对于 MaxST 下界则多项式时间可解，二者形成鲜明对比。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了加权 MAS 问题上 MaxST 超越保证的参数化可解性，是有向图加权问题中难得的正面"超越保证"结果。它打开了 MaxST 与有向圈结构关联、完美图理论在 FPT 算法设计应用等独立图论问题的新研究方向，并为研究"超越保证"参数化范式在加权场景下的能力边界提供了重要参考。

Abstract: We study the weighted case of the \textsc{Maximum Acyclic Subgraph (MAS)} problem, where each edge of a given directed graph has a positive weight assigned, and the task is to find a maximum-weight acyclic edge set. The famous and well-studied random ordering lower bound guarantees the existence of an acyclic set that gives at least the half of the total edge weight. The maximum spanning tree (MaxST) guarantee, which is the weight of a maximum-weight acyclic subgraph of the underlying undirected graph of $G$, is another natural lower bound for the weight of an acyclic subgraph. A solution of this weight dominates the random ordering solution on instances where MaxST spans the most of the total edge weight. Our main contribution are two parameterized algorithms that find acyclic subgraphs of total weight larger than the weight of the MaxST of $G$. Both our algorithms find a solution of total weight at least $MaxST(G)+k$, for a given integer $k\ge 0$, or report that it does not exist, and first of our algorithms runs in time $2^{k^{\mathcal{O}(1)}}\cdot \mathcal{I}^{\mathcal{O}(1)}$ and works when all weights are integers; our second algorithm handles rational weights not less than $1$, and its running time is upper-bounded by $n^{k^{\mathcal{O}(1)}}\cdot \mathcal{I}^{\mathcal{O}(1)}$. This positive result is rather surprising since solving \textsc{MAS} above the random ordering lower bound is \classNP-hard in the same rational weights scenario, when $k=1$. Our findings unravel intricate connections between structure of MaxSTs and directed cycles, use perfect graph theorem to tackle rational weights, and raise graph-theoretic questions that are interesting on their own. Of another importance, this is one of the few examples of positive ``above guarantee'' results for a weighted problem on directed graphs, especially for rational weights.

</details>
